Function grad_smooth(h,n,alpha,p=None):
- c = compute_c5_vec_fft(h,n)
- m=c.max(); arr=alpha*(c-m); if max(arr)>50? weights stable: shifted=arr-arr.max(); w=exp(shifted); w/=sum.
- If p maybe select top p: We can set c not top? For weights all with softmax; small c weights negligible. That's fine; gradient cost independent of active. Use all w (length 2n-1). FFT conv cost independent of number active, so no need top. weights for all lags; many tiny but okay. For numerical, set w[w<1e-30]=0 maybe.
- grad = compute_grad_weights(w,h,n)
- return grad, c, m
But if alpha very high, weights may underflow to zero for non-max, effectively top active. Good.

compute_grad_weights(w,h,n):
- D=2n-2.
- S via cumsum of w: pref=np.concatenate(([0],cumsum(w))). For i array? S_i = pref[D - i +1] - pref[n-1 - i] for i=0..n-1. Vectorize: i=np.arange(n); S = pref[D+1 - i] - pref[n-1 - i]. Check D=2n-2, D+1=2n-1 pref length 2n. For i=0: pref[2n-1]-pref[n-1] sum indices n-1..2n-2 length n. ok. i=n-1: pref[n]-pref[0] sum 0..n-1. ok.
- kernel_complex = w[::-1] + 1j*w (since conv with w[::-1] real C1, with w imag C2). Note conv linear length 3n-2. M next power of 2 >= 3n-2. H=fft(h,M); K=fft(kernel_complex,M); conv=ifft(H*K). c1=conv[n-1:n-1+n].real; c2=conv[n-1:n-1+n].imag. grad = S - c1 - c2. Return grad.
Potential numerical: weights sum1; S maybe O(1); c1,c2 O? h in [0,1], weights sum1, c1 sum over p h_p w_{p-i}; O1. grad O few. Good.
Check for h constant 0.5, weights symmetric? c all 0.5? Actually c max at 0? For constant, c(m)=length*0.25, max k=0 length n; c0=0.5, others smaller. weights not symmetric? α high puts weight on k=0, grad=1-2h=0, okay. If multiple.

Let's design projected gradient carefully:
```
def projected_gradient(h,n,deadline,alpha=500,lr=0.05, max_iter=5000):
  h=project; best_c=compute_c5; curr_c=best_c; curr=h
  momentum=0.9? y=0
  for it:
    grad,c,curr_c=grad_smooth_true(...)
    # center: g = grad - grad.mean() (since feasible sum)
    g = grad - np.mean(grad)
    # normalize by sqrt? Step size based on Lipschitz? Use adaptive.
    step = lr
    improved=False
    for _ in range(20):
       cand = project(h - step*g, n)
       c = compute_c5(cand,n)  # true
       if c < curr_c - 1e-15:
          h=cand; curr_c=c; if c<best: best=...
          improved=True; break
       step*=0.5
    if not improved:
       # maybe increase alpha to focus active set and try
       alpha*=1.5; lr*=0.5
       if alpha>50000: alpha=50000
    # optionally use line search on surrogate: compute g and use c? But true checks many.
```
True eval per backtrack. If no improvement, many iterations stop. Could be slow if lr too small. We can use surrogate acceptance to update h then check true best. Let's make:
- Compute smooth surrogate objective value val and grad (with alpha). This already computed c/m for weights. We need val = LSE(c) (dx scaled). Use val for line search: choose step minimizing val(cand) maybe using quadratic approx. Since val expensive too (c via FFT). But true C is easy from c. We can accept if val_cand < val - 1e-16*... and then if true C < curr_true or global? Because val approximates true only if active weights. If non-active shifts become max, true may increase. We can maintain a ceiling: require true_cand <= curr_true + 1e-12 to not worsen current. But maybe sometimes allowing slight true increase helps escape? Nonconvex; could accept uphill in true if surrogate down? Might get stuck. Use sa? Simpler: require true C ≤ curr_true for current update; global best updated if lower. If stuck, perturbation.

def lbfgs_fun(x,n,alpha,mu):
  c=corr_vec(x,n)
  m=c.max(); a=alpha*(c-m); amax=a.max()
  if amax>500: val=m+(amax + log(sum(exp(a-amax))))/alpha
  else: val=m + (log(sum(exp(a))))/alpha? Wait if m is max not subtract? For stable: shifted=alpha*(c-m). shifted_max=shifted.max(); logsum=shifted_max + log(sum(exp(shifted-shifted_max))). val=m + logsum/alpha. Since m=c.max(), logsum/alpha = logsumexp(alpha(c-m))/alpha. If shifted all <=0. Good.
  diff=x.sum()-n/2
  val += mu*diff*diff
  return val

def lbfgs_jac(x,n,alpha,mu):
  c=corr_vec(x,n)
  m=c.max(); a=alpha*(c-m); amax=a.max(); w=exp(a-amax); w/=w.sum()
  g=grad_from_weights(w,x,n)  # includes dx
  if mu: g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
  return g
```

Algorithm:
```
def projected_gradient(h,n,deadline,alpha=1000,lr_init=None,max_iter=20000):
 h=project; curr=corr; best=curr; best_h=h; lr=lr_init or 0.05; stuck=0
 for it in range(max_iter):
   if time>deadline: break
   val, grad, c = lse_val_grad(h,n,alpha)
   m=c.max(); curr=compute_c5(c,n) # or m
   # center grad
   g=grad - grad.mean()
   gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
   if gn <1e-14: break
   g = g/gn
   # step = lr /? gradient norm normalized so step is delta in h units. Use lr ~0.01-0.1.
   step=lr
   improved=False
   for _ in range(30):
      cand=project(h - step*g,n)
      cat=corr_vec(cand,n); ct=cat.max()
      if ct <= curr - 1e-16: # allow equal?
          h=cand; curr=ct; if ct<best: best=...
          improved=True; break
      # also if ct<best? update but not accept? maybe
      step*=0.5
   if improved:
      stuck=0; lr=min(0.5, lr*1.05)
   else:
      stuck+=1; lr*=0.5; alpha*=1.3
      if alpha>50000: alpha=50000
      if lr<1e-12: break
      # if stuck many, perturb
      if stuck%20==0:
         h_pert = project(h + 1e-3*rng.normal, n)
         # accept if not too worse? To escape, set h=h_pert, curr=... but best kept
         c_p=corr; if c_p < curr + 0.01: h=h_pert; curr=c_p
```
Using normalized gradient and step in h units. Initial lr maybe 0.05? If gradient normalized, step 0.05 moves L2 by 0.05, okay. But objective scale dx ~0.006; maybe too small? We can adapt. For n=300, dx=0.0066; moving 0.05 can change C significantly. Try lr=0.02. L-BFGS maybe better.
Number iterations: each iter one grad (FFT) + few evals. For n=1000, maybe 5k iterations in 100s? maybe. Use time.

`project_h(z,n)`:
```
def project_h(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    s = z.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-13:
        return z
    lo = -1.0; hi=1.0
    # ensure monotone? We can tighten
    # If target < z.sum(): need negative lambda. lo maybe min(0, target - max(z)*n?) but -1 enough.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       y=np.clip(z+mid,0.0,1.0)
       ys=y.sum()
       if ys < target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    y=np.clip(z+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust tiny residual exactly by distributing to elements not at bounds
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # find non-bound for first/last
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(y<1.0-1e-15)[0]
          if len(idx): y[idx[0]] += diff / len? maybe just y[idx[0]] = min(1,y[0]+diff)
       else:
          idx=np.where(y>1e-15)[0]
          if len(idx): y[idx[0]] += diff
       # final if not exact due float, scale nonbounds
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    # If sum still off, do iterative small adjust on a non-bound
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          for i in range(n):
             if y[i] < 1.0:
                add = min(diff, 1-y[i]); y[i]+=add; diff-=add
                if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       else:
          for i in range(n):
             if y[i] > 0.0:
                sub = min(-diff, y[i]); y[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
                if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return y
```
Project by constant λ is not necessarily exact after clipping due numeric; final adjust. Good.

`corr_vec(h,n)` using FFT:
```
def corr_vec(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    need=2*n-1
    M=1
    while M < need: M <<=1
    b=(1.0-h)[::-1]
    H=np.fft.rfft(h, M)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b, M)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(H*B, M)[:need]
    c=conv[::-1]
    return c*dx
```
Need ensure h 1D contiguous. For n small, FFT overhead maybe but okay. Could use direct for n<128? But no need.
Numerical: conv may have tiny negative; c max unaffected.

`c5_h(h,n): return float(np.max(corr_vec(h,n)))`.

`weights_from_c(c,alpha)`:
```
def softmax_c(c,alpha):
    m=c.max()
    a=alpha*(c-m)
    am=a.max()
    w=np.exp(a-am)
    s=w.sum()
    if s==0 or not finite: w=np.zeros_like(c); w[m==c.max()]=?; w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
Since a<=0, am=0 (if any max). Use `a = alpha*(c - c.max())` all ≤0; then no need am? If c max exactly, a max=0. But if alpha large and c differences up to 1, a min -large; exp underflows okay. `w=np.exp(a); w /= w.sum()`. If all underflow? max exp=1 so sum≥1. Good. Use clip maybe.

`grad_from_weights(w,h,n)`:
```
D=2*n-2
M=1; while M<3*n-2: M<<=1
# S
pref=np.empty(D+2? Actually w length D+1=2n-1.
pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w))) # length 2n
i=np.arange(n)
S = pref[D+1 - i] - pref[n-1 - i]  # D+1=2n-1
# convolve
wr = w[::-1]
H = np.fft.rfft(h,M)
# pack? Use separate
conv1=np.fft.irfft(H * np.fft.rfft(wr,M), M)
c1=conv1[n-1:n-1+n]
wr2=w
conv2=np.fft.irfft(H * np.fft.rfft(wr2,M), M)
c2=conv2[n-1:n-1+n]
grad = (S - c1 - c2)*(2.0/n)
return grad
```
Need check `pref` indices: length w+1 =2n. For i=0: D+1-i =2n-1, index max; pref[2n-1] sum w[0..2n-2], pref[n-1]. sum idx n-1..2n-2. For i=n-1: 2n-1-(n-1)=n, pref[n] - pref[0]. good.
Complex pack to reduce? We can implement two rffts. But note H reused. Good.

`lse_value_grad(h,n,alpha)` returns val, grad, c:
```
def lse_value_grad(h,n,alpha):
    c=corr_vec(h,n)
    m=c.max()
    a=alpha*(c-m)
    # stable logsum: a <=0
    logsum = a.max() + log(sum(exp(a-a.max())))  # a.max()=0
    val = m + logsum/alpha
    w=np.exp(a)
    w /= w.sum()
    grad = grad_from_weights(w,h,n)
    return float(val), grad, c
```
Since a.max()=0, logsum=log(sum(exp(a))). For alpha small, okay. If alpha*range > 1000, exp(-1000) underflow but max=1. Use if `np.max(-a) > 50`? No need; sum exp underflow to 1 for only max, okay. log(1)=0. Use `np.log(np.sum(np.exp(a)))`; if sum=1 fine. If due floating a has tiny positive >0 because c-m? m=c.max exactly, a≤0 maybe tiny positive due rounding? Set `a = alpha*(c - m); a = np.maximum(a, -1000); a -= a.max()` to be safe. Then logsum = a.max()+log(sum). val=m+logsum/alpha.

`lbfgs_fun` and `lbfgs_grad`:
```
def lbfgs_fun(x,n,alpha,mu):
  val,_,_ = lse_value_grad(x,n,alpha)
  diff=x.sum()-0.5*n
  return val + mu*diff*diff
def lbfgs_jac(x,n,alpha,mu):
  _,g,_=lse_value_grad(x,n,alpha)
  if mu: g = g + 2.0*mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)
  return g
```
This calls lse twice; duplicate c. Could optimize by custom_caching with global? Maybe not. We can write separate fun/jac that compute c once each. But scipy calls fun then jac same x; duplicate cost maybe double. For n up to 1200, still okay. But for many iterations, duplicate. Could use a mutable global cache keyed by object id? Risk. Since top-level, we can define a simple global `_CACHE = {'key': None, 'val': None, 'grad': None, 'c': None}` and in fun/jac store. But scipy may call fun and jac with same x array object? It passes same x to fun then jac; we can cache based on id(x) and maybe hash? But x can be modified? Not. Simpler: top-level functions `lbfgs_fun_and_jac`? L-BFGS-B doesn't accept both. Could implement our own L-BFGS? Not.
We can avoid penalty and use SLSQP? SLSQP also duplicate. Maybe okay. FFT cost dominates but double maybe acceptable. For n=1200, 4k FFT maybe 0.1ms? fine.
Let's keep simple.

```
def optimize_lbfgs(...):
  h=project_h(h,n); best_h=h; best_c=c5_h
  bounds=[(0,1)]*n
  for alpha in alphas:
    if time>deadline: break
    # allocate per alpha: maybe 60s or maxiter limit
    try:
      res=minimize(lbfgs_fun, h, jac=lbfgs_jac, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiters,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':40})
      h_new=project_h(res.x,n); c=c5_h(h_new,n)
      if c<best_c: best_h=h_new; best_c=c
      else:
         # still update h to res? maybe keep best_h for next alpha? If res not improve true but smooth better, using res may be okay. We can set h=project(res.x) if finite and c <= best_c+0.01? To avoid worsening.
      h=project_h(h_new,n) maybe best? For next alpha, start from best_h or res? Usually from res (smooth) continue. But if true worse, maybe use best. We'll set h=h_new (res) if c <= best_c + 1e-9 else best_h.
    except: pass
    h = project_h(h,n)
    if time>deadline: break
  return best_h,best_c
```

L-BFGS line search uses penalized objective, will keep sum near target if mu large. Choose mu=1.0 for n=300? Gradient penalty 2*diff; if diff ~1, grad 2 vs objective grad 0.006, strong. May slow. def _positive_compose(total, parts):
   if parts==1: return [total]
   cuts=np.sort(rng.randint(1,total,parts-1))
   pieces=np.diff(np.concatenate(([0],cuts,[total])))
   return pieces.tolist()
```

```

 while time<deadline:
   improved=False
   for i in range(n):
     if time>deadline: return h,best
     lo=max(0,i-radius); hi=min(n,i+radius+1)
     for j in range(lo,hi):
       if i==j: continue
       # move +d from j to i
       if h[i]+d<=1 and h[j]-d>=0:

           c=c5_h(h,n)

           else: h[i]=oldi; h[j]=oldj
       # move -d
       if h[i]-d>=0 and h[j]+d<=1:
           ...
   if not improved: break
```

`projected_gradient` should use true c as current. It may get stuck; we can include periodic annealing perturb. But if it perturbs, best_h kept. In run, projected_gradient returns best_h,best_c (not necessarily current). It can update current to perturbed for exploration. Good.

Pseudo `projected_gradient`:
```
def projected_gradient(h,n,deadline,alpha=2000,lr=0.03,max_iter=1000000):
    h=project_h(h,n); curr=c5_h; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=curr
    rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    stagnant=0
    while time < deadline:
       val,g,c = lse_value_grad(h,n,alpha)
       curr = float(c.max()) # c from val
       if curr < best_c - 1e-15: best_c=curr; best_h=h.copy()
       g = g - g.mean()
       gn = np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-13: break
       g = g/gn
       try step=lr; ok=False
       for _ in range(25):
           cand=project_h(h - step*g, n)
           cc = c5_h(cand,n)
           if cc <= curr + 1e-13:  # allow small? Require true not worse? Use cc < curr - 1e-16 maybe.
               # For descent, require cc < curr to avoid no progress. Use cc < curr - 1e-15
               h=cand; curr=cc; ok=True; break
           step *=0.5
       if not ok:
           stagnant +=1
           # try a smaller random move along active gradient? Or perturb if stagnant
           if stagnant > 20:
              # anneal: try random feasible perturbation, accept if not too worse than best? 
              pert = project_h(h + 0.01*rng.normal(size=n), n)
              cp = c5_h(pert,n)
              if cp < best_c + 0.05: # allow explore
                  h=pert; curr=cp
              stagnant=0
           lr *=0.5
           alpha *= 1.5
          if alpha>20000: alpha=20000
           if lr < 1e-10: break
       else:
           stagnant=0
           lr = min(0.2, lr*1.1)
    return best_h,best_c
```
Require cc < curr? If equal, no progress. Use `cc < curr - 1e-15`. But if gradient direction very small, may fail. Use also if cc < best_c? update best, but current not update. Exploration perturb handles.
The line search using true C may be expensive 25 evals per iter. But with FFT for n<=1000 ~0.5ms, 25*1000=25s. Limit max_iter/time. We can reduce max backtracks 12 and step factor 0.6. Also use surrogate val line search? But true is fine.
Maybe optimize by using c from lse_value_grad for current. For candidate, we can compute `corr_vec` directly; c5_h. Good.

For n high and lr normalized, step maybe too large/small. We can set lr based on dx? Since h values changes, global optimum moves; lr 0.05. We can adapt: if no improvement, halve; if improvement, increase up to 0.3. But too large may cause max increase; backtrack handles.
Use `lr_init = 0.05 if n<500 else 0.02`. In `projected_gradient`, lr initially given.

def f_k_val_grad(k, h, n):
   dx=2.0/n
   if k>=0:
     kk=k; nv=n-kk
     if nv<=0: return 0.0, zeros (not)
     val=dx*np.sum(h[:nv]*(1-h[kk:]))
     g=np.zeros(n); g[:nv]+=dx*(1-h[kk:]); g[kk:]+= -dx*h[:nv]

     val=dx*np.sum(h[kk:]*(1-h[:nv]))
     g=np.zeros(n); g[kk:]+=dx*(1-h[:nv]); g[:nv]+=-dx*h[kk:]

```

Detailed code for `optimize_lbfgs`:
```
def optimize_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,alphas,mu,base_iter):
    h = project_h(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c5_from_h(h,n)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    current_h = h.copy()
    current_c = best_c
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline: break
        maxit = base_iter
        try:
            res = minimize(lbfgs_obj, current_h, jac=lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu),
                           method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                           options={'maxiter':maxit, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':50})
            cand = project_h(res.x,n)
            cc = c5_from_h(cand,n)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c=cc; best_h=cand.copy()
            # choose current for next alpha
            if cc <= current_c + 0.02:
                current_h = cand
                current_c = cc
            else:
                # maybe keep current_h
                pass
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c
```
This may ignore a smooth improvement that worsens true by 0.01 but could lead to lower later. Maybe allow 0.03. Use 0.03.
But if first low alpha worsens 0.02, current switches; maybe not. Could set current to cand if finite regardless? Starting next alpha from smoothed may be better. But if cand is constant C=0.5, high alpha may still recover? Maybe, but risk. Use condition `cc < best_c + 0.05`.
`base_iter`: We can call with `min(80, max(20, int((deadline-time.time())*?)))`? Not know. Use base_iter from caller.

  ...

`active_slqsp`:
```
def active_slqsp(h,n,deadline,active_tol=0.005,max_active=8,maxiter=300):
  h=project; best...
  c=corr_vec(h,n); m=c.max()
  idxs=np.where(c >= m - active_tol)[0]
  # sort by c descending
  if len(idxs)>max_active:
     order=np.argsort(c[idxs])[::-1]; idxs=idxs[order[:max_active]]
  else:
     idxs=idxs[np.argsort(c[idxs])[::-1]]
  ks=idxs - (n-1)
  x0=np.empty(n+1); x0[0]=m+1e-6; x0[1:]=h
  bounds=[(0.0,2.0)] + [(0.0,1.0)]*n
  cons=[{'type':'eq',...}, {'type':'ineq','fun':active_ineq,'jac':active_ineq_jac,'args':(n,ks)}]
  res=minimize(active_obj,x0,args=(n,),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
  cand=project_h(res.x[1:],n); cc=...
```

def make_block_binary(n,rng,b=None):
  if n%2: # for odd, make even then project
  ones=n//2; zeros=n-ones (if odd zeros=ceil)
  if b is None:
    maxb=min(60, ones-1, zeros-1)
    if maxb <1: maxb=1
    b=rng.randint(1, maxb+1)
  b=min(b,ones,zeros-1 if zeros>0 else 1)
  if b<1: b=1
  # positive pieces
  blocks=positive_compose(ones,b)
  gaps=positive_compose(zeros,b+1) if zeros >= b+1 else ...
```
Need `positive_compose(total, parts)` top-level. If total < parts, set parts=total (gaps 1). For zeros maybe if zeros < b+1, reduce b or allow zero gaps. Simpler: if zeros < b+1, b = max(1, zeros-1). Then if zeros==0? n? no.

`positive_compose` using rng. Need if total=0? not for ones. For gaps zeros maybe >0. If parts=1 return [total]. If total < parts: return [1]*total + zeros? But positive parts impossible; reduce parts. We'll ensure parts ≤ total.
```
def positive_compose(total,parts,rng):
  if parts <=0: return []
  if parts > total: parts = total if total>0 else 1
  if parts ==1: return [total]
  cuts=np.sort(rng.randint(1,total,parts-1))
  arr=np.concatenate(([0],cuts,[total]))
  return np.diff(arr).astype(int).tolist()
```
If total int. `rng.randint(1,total,parts-1)` excludes total, good. If total=1 parts=1.
After blocks/gaps lists sums maybe total. Fill.

y=np.zeros(n)
for j in range(3):
  f=rng.randint(1,6)
  ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
  a=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
  y += a*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
med=np.median(y)
h=(y>med).astype(float)

`polish_candidate`:
Need decide quick/full.
```
def polish_candidate(h,n,deadline,rng,quick=False):
    h=project_h(h,n); c=c5_from_h(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    if quick:
       # one L-BFGS alpha 1000 maxiter 30, PG alpha 2000 time 30s
       if n <= 1500 and time < deadline-5:
          d1=min(deadline-5, time.time()+ (30 if n<=400 else 15))
          h2,c2=optimize_lbfgs(h,n,d1,[500,2000],mu=10,base_iter=30)
          update
       # PG
       d2=time.time()+ (20 if n<=500 else 10)
       h2,c2=projected_gradient(h,n,min(deadline,d2),alpha=3000,lr=0.03,rng)
       update
    else:
       # L-BFGS alphas [50,200,800,3000,10000]? allocate until deadline maybe 50% of passed? But polish doesn't know total? It gets deadline for this candidate.
       # Use first 40% for lbfgs, 40% PG, 20 SLSQP/pair
       t_now=time.time()
       remaining=deadline-t_now
       if remaining<=0: return best
       lb_deadline = t_now + remaining*0.35
       alphas=[50,200,800,3000,10000]
       base_iter=70 if n<=350 else 40 if n<=800 else 20
       h2,c2=optimize_lbfgs(h,n,lb_deadline,alphas,10,base_iter)
       update
       t_now=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t_now
       if remaining>10:
          pg_deadline = t_now + remaining*0.45
          # PG low then high
          d1 = t_now + (remaining*0.25)
          h3,c3=projected_gradient(h,n,d1,500,0.03,rng)
          update
          d2 = pg_deadline
          h3,c3=projected_gradient(h,n,d2,8000,0.02,rng)
          update
       t_now=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t_now
       if n<=350 and remaining>15:
          d1 = t_now + min(20, remaining*0.4)
          h4,c4=active_slqsp(h,n,d1,0.005,8,300)
          update
          if time<deadline-5:
             h4,c4=optimize_slqsp? maybe skip
       if n<=450 and remaining>8:
          h5,c5=local_pair_search(h,n,deadline,[0.01,0.005,0.002], radius=6 if n<=350 else 4)
          update
    return best_h,best_c
```

Let's detail `corr_vec`:
```
def corr_vec(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    dx=2.0/n
    need=2*n-1
    if n <= 600:
       return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * dx
    M=1
    while M < need:
       M <<= 1
    a=h
    b=(1.0-h)[::-1]
    H=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,M)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(H*B,M)
    c=conv[:need][::-1]
    return c*dx
```
`conv[:need]` important because length M with small roundoff tail. Good.

`grad_from_weights` if n maybe 1? For n<2 return zeros. But n≥2. Use `M=1; while M < 3*n-2: M <<=1`. If `3*n-2` maybe 4 for n=2. M power-of-two. Use rfft of w length 2n-1 padded to M.
```
def grad_from_weights(w,h,n):
  dx=2.0/n
  D=2*n-2
  L=len(w)
  if L != D+1: w=np.zeros(D+1); ...? weights should.
  H=np.fft.rfft(h,M)
  wr=w[::-1]
  W1=np.fft.rfft(wr,M)
  W2=np.fft.rfft(w,M)
  conv1=np.fft.irfft(H*W1,M)
  conv2=np.fft.irfft(H*W2,M)
  c1=conv1[n-1:n-1+n]
  c2=conv2[n-1:n-1+n]
  pref=...
  S=...
  return (S - c1 - c2)*dx
```
Should we zero tiny imaginary? irfft returns real.

def lse_value_grad(h,n,alpha):
    c=corr_vec(h,n)
    m=float(c.max())
    if alpha <=0: alpha=1.0
    a=alpha*(c-m)
    a=np.maximum(a, -745.0)  # exp underflow limit
    amax=a.max()
    expa=np.exp(a-amax)
    s=expa.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       logsum=amax
    else:
       logsum=amax+np.log(s)
    val=m + logsum/alpha
    w=expa/s if finite else zeros max
    if w.sum() !=1: ...
    grad=grad_from_weights(w,h,n)
    return val, grad, c
```
Since a max 0, amax 0. Use -745 to avoid underflow warnings? `np.exp` of -1000 gives 0 no warning? Maybe underflow warning? Usually no. Use clip to -700.

```
def lbfgs_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
   try:
    val,_,_=lse_value_grad(x,n,alpha)
   except: return 1e100
   diff=x.sum()-0.5*n
   return float(val + mu*diff*diff/(n*n))
```

def polish_candidate(h,n,deadline,rng,quick=False):
    h=project_h(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_from_h(h,n)
    t=time.time()
    remaining=deadline-t
    if remaining<=0: return ...
    if quick:
       # LBFGS 30%
       dL=t+remaining*0.4
       al=[300,1500] if n<=500 else [1000]
       base=25 if n<=350 else 15
       h2,c2=optimize_lbfgs(h,n,dL,al,10,base)
       update
       t=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t
       if remaining>5:
          dP=t+remaining*0.6
          h2,c2=projected_gradient(h,n,dP,3000,0.03,rng)
          update
       return best
    else:
       # LBFGS 35%
       dL=t+remaining*0.35
       base=80 if n<=350 else 45 if n<=800 else 25
       al=[50,200,800,3000,10000] if n<=500 else [200,1000,5000]
       h2,c2=optimize_lbfgs(h,n,dL,al,10,base)
       update
       t=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t
       if remaining>8:
          dP1=t+remaining*0.3
          h2,c2=projected_gradient(h,n,dP1,800,0.03,rng)
          update
          t=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t
       if remaining>8:
          dP2=t+remaining*0.5
          h2,c2=projected_gradient(h,n,dP2,8000,0.02,rng)
          update
       t=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t
       if n<=350 and remaining>12:
          dA=t+min(25,remaining*0.4)
          h2,c2=active_slqsp(h,n,dA,0.005,8,250)
          update
          t=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t
       if n<=450 and remaining>8:
          h2,c2=local_pair_search(h,n,deadline,[0.01,0.005,0.002], radius=6 if n<=350 else 4)
          update
       return best
```

Hill-climbing details:
```
def binary_hill_climb(h,n,deadline,rng,step=0.05? binary):
 h=project_h(h,n)
 # ensure binary by threshold and adjust count
 h = make_binary_from_float(h,n)
 c=c5_from_h; target? We can return best.
 best_c=c; best_h=h
 no_improve=0
 while time<deadline:
   improved=False
   # generate random pairs until find improvement, max scans
   ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0]
   # try a number of random swaps based on time
   attempts = 5000
   for _ in range(attempts):
      if time>deadline: break
      i=rng.choice(ones); j=rng.choice(zeros)
      # swap
      h[i]=0; h[j]=1
      c=c5_from_h(h,n)
      if c < best_c - 1e-15:
         best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True; no_improve=0
         # continue scanning maybe multiple improvements in same pass
      else:
         # accept uphill with probability if not too high? For annealing
         if c < best_c + 0.01 and rng.rand() < step:
            h[i]=0; h[j]=1
         else:
            h[i]=1; h[j]=0
   if not improved:
      no_improve +=1
      # perturb: k random swaps (if no improvement)
      for _ in range(5): random swap accept regardless
      no_improve reset? 
      if no_improve > 30: break
```
However `ones`/`zeros` lists become stale after swaps; update if length changed? We can regenerate each pass.
This random pair search might be slow due c eval Python. But 5000 eval per pass * maybe 0.5ms=2.5s per pass; 20 passes=50s. Good. It can explore.

- Get top active lags (say 20). For each 1 position i, for each active lag k, positions j=i+k (if zero) or i-k (if zero) are 0 causing active count; swapping i to 0 and that 0 to1? def make_binary(h,n):
  h=(h>0.5).astype(float)
  target=n//2
  cnt=h.sum()
  while cnt>target: flip random 1 to0
  while cnt<target: flip random 0 to1
  return h
```

`make_binary(h,n,rng)`:
```
def make_binary(h,n,rng):
    if n <=0: return h
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    # threshold at 0.5, but use values to choose flips
    b=(h>=0.5).astype(float)
    target=n//2
    cnt=int(b.sum())
    if n%2:
       # make one 0.5? But binary search even only. If odd, set target floor and later project? Better return project_h after adjusting to floor? For binary search skip odd.
    if cnt > target:
       ones=np.where(b>0.5)[0]
       # flip lowest h? Use rng permutation
       perm=rng.permutation(len(ones))
       for idx in perm[cnt-target:]:
          b[ones[idx]]=0
    elif cnt < target:
       zeros=np.where(b<0.5)[0]
       need=target-cnt
       perm=rng.permutation(len(zeros))
       for idx in perm[:need]: b[zeros[idx]]=1
    return b
```
If n odd, target floor, sum not n/2; but binary search should only call even. For safety, after make_binary call project_h? That may introduce .5; but then not binary. We can in `binary_active_search`: if n%2==1, return project_h(c5). But maybe for odd n, use target rounded: make floor and add one 0.5? Objective with one .5 can be non-integer. Not necessary.

def binary_score_vec(h,n):
    if n <= 900:
       return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    c=corr_vec(h,n); return c*(n/2.0)
```

`binary_active_search`:
```
def binary_active_search(h,n,deadline,rng,max_active=16,max_candidates=4000,random_trials=30):
    if n % 2 == 1 or n < 20:
       h=project_h(h,n); return h, c5_from_h(h,n)
    h=make_binary(h,n,rng).copy()
    h=project_h(h,n) # exact sum (already)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_from_h(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    stuck=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       sv = binary_score_vec(h,n)
       M = sv.max()
       # if current is best? h current may not be best. We only improve current if better than best? Actually we keep current as search state; best_h separate.
       tol = max(1.0, 0.03*M)
       active_mask = sv >= M - tol
       if not np.any(active_mask): active_mask[0]=True
       idx = np.where(active_mask)[0]
       if len(idx) > max_active:
          order = np.argsort(sv[idx])[::-1]
          idx = idx[order[:max_active]]
       ks = idx - (n-1)
       ones = np.where(h>0.5)[0]
       cand_set=set()
       for i in ones:
          for k in ks:
             kk=int(k)
             j=i+kk
             if 0 <= j < n and h[j] < 0.5:
                cand_set.add(i*n+j)
             j=i-kk
             if 0 <= j < n and h[j] < 0.5:
                cand_set.add(i*n+j)
       if len(cand_set) == 0:
          # random perturb
          ...
       cand_list = list(cand_set)
       if len(cand_list) > max_candidates:
          cand_list = rng.choice(cand_list, size=max_candidates, replace=False).tolist()
       else:
          rng.shuffle(cand_list)
       improved=False
       for code in cand_list:
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          i=code//n; j=code%n
          if h[i] > 0.5 and h[j] < 0.5:
             h[i]=0.0; h[j]=1.0
             s = float(np.max(binary_score_vec(h,n)))
             cs = s*dx
             if cs < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=cs; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True
             else:
                # sometimes accept to current if not too worse (plateau)
                if cs <= best_c + dx*0.5 and rng.rand() < 0.2:
                   # keep swap in current
                   pass
                else:
                   h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0
          # If accepted a non-best swap, current changed but best same; okay.
       if improved:
          stuck=0
          # continue; current h may include non-improving swaps? We only keep improving swaps, not random accepted? Actually if cs not best but accepted plateau, h remains swapped. That could help. Good.
          # We may want to set h to best_h if best improved? Current includes swap.
          if time.time() + 5 > deadline: break
       else:
          stuck += 1
          # Try best among several random single swaps from current (or best_h?)
          base = h if (best_h is None) else best_h.copy()? Use current.
          # evaluate random swap candidates
          best_rand_h = h.copy(); best_rand_c = c5_from_h(h,n)
          ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0]
          for _ in range(random_trials):
             if time.time() >= deadline: break
             ii=int(rng.choice(ones)); jj=int(rng.choice(zeros))
             h2=h.copy()
             h2[ii]=0; h2[jj]=1
             c2=c5_from_h(h2,n)
             if c2 < best_rand_c:
                best_rand_c=c2; best_rand_h=h2
          # accept perturbation if not too worse than best_c
          if best_rand_c < best_c + 3*dx:
             h=best_rand_h
          else:
             # do a random double swap to escape
             for _ in range(3): random swap h
          if stuck > 50:
             # bigger perturbation: threshold a noisy version of best
             h=make_binary(best_h + 0.3*rng.normal(size=n), n, rng)
             stuck=0
          if stuck > 200:
             break
    return best_h, best_c
```

def make_block_binary(n,rng,b=None):
  ones=n//2; zeros=n-ones
  if ones <=0: return project_h(np.zeros(n),n)
  maxb=min(60, ones)
  if b is None: b=rng.randint(1,maxb+1) if maxb>0 else 1
  b=max(1,min(int(b),ones))
  blocks=positive_compose(ones,b,rng)
  gaps=compose_nonneg(zeros,b+1,rng)
  h=np.zeros(n); pos=0
  for idx in range(b):
     if pos < n: pos += gaps[idx]
     if pos + blocks[idx] > n: blocks[idx]=max(0,n-pos); # but sum may off
     h[pos:pos+blocks[idx]] = 1.0; pos += blocks[idx]
  # if pos<n leftover gap
  h=project_h(h[:n],n)
  return h
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_h(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size != n:
        z = z.reshape(n)
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)

    target = 0.5 * n
    s = float(z.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-14:
        return z

    if s < target:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(z))
    else:
        lo = -float(np.max(z))
        hi = 0.0
    if hi <= lo:
        hi = lo + 1e-12

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip(z + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        if y.sum() < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    y = np.clip(z + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    resid = target - float(y.sum())
    if resid > 0.0:
        for i in range(n):
            if y[i] < 1.0:
                inc = min(resid, 1.0 - y[i])
                y[i] += inc
                resid -= inc
                if abs(resid) <= 1e-15:
                    break
    elif resid < 0.0:
        for i in range(n):
            if y[i] > 0.0:
                dec = min(-resid, y[i])
                y[i] -= dec
                resid += dec
                if abs(resid) <= 1e-15:
                    break

    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    resid = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(resid) > 1e-15:
        if resid > 0.0:
            for i in range(n):
                if y[i] < 1.0:
                    y[i] = min(1.0, y[i] + resid / max(1, n - i))
        else:
            for i in range(n):
                if y[i] > 0.0:
                    y[i] = max(0.0, y[i] + resid / max(1, n - i))
    return y


def corr_vec(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    need = 2 * n - 1

    if n <= 650:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx

    M = 1
    while M < need:
        M <<= 1

    a = h
    b = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * B, M)
    return conv[:need][::-1] * dx


def c5_from_h(h, n):
    c = corr_vec(h, n)
    return float(np.max(c))


def f_k_val_grad(h, n, k):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    k = int(k)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if k >= 0:
        nv = n - k
        if nv <= 0:
            return 0.0, g
        val = dx * float(np.sum(h[0:nv] * (1.0 - h[k:n])))
        g[0:nv] += dx * (1.0 - h[k:n])
        g[k:n] += -dx * h[0:nv]
    else:
        kk = -k
        nv = n - kk
        if nv <= 0:
            return 0.0, g
        val = dx * float(np.sum(h[kk:n] * (1.0 - h[0:nv])))
        g[kk:n] += dx * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
        g[0:nv] += -dx * h[kk:n]

    return val, g


def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    a = alpha * (c - m)
    a = np.maximum(a, -700.0)

    amax = float(np.max(a))
    e = np.exp(a - amax)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[np.where(c == m)[0]] = 1.0
        w /= np.max(w)
        return w
    return e / s


def grad_from_weights(w, h, n):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    D = 2 * n - 2
    if len(w) != D + 1:
        ww = np.zeros(D + 1, dtype=float)
        nuse = min(len(w), D + 1)
        ww[:nuse] = w[:nuse]
        w = ww

    M = 1
    while M < 3 * n - 2:
        M <<= 1

    pref = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    i = np.arange(n)
    S = pref[D + 1 - i] - pref[n - 1 - i]

    H = np.fft.rfft(h, M)
    Wr = w[::-1]
    conv1 = np.fft.irfft(H * np.fft.rfft(Wr, M), M)
    conv2 = np.fft.irfft(H * np.fft.rfft(w, M), M)

    c1 = conv1[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    c2 = conv2[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    return (S - c1 - c2) * dx


def lse_value_grad(h, n, alpha):
    c = corr_vec(h, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        alpha = 1.0

    a = alpha * (c - m)
    a = np.maximum(a, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(a)
    s = float(e.sum())

    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        logsum = float(np.log(s))
        w = e / s
    else:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        logsum = 0.0

    val = float(m + logsum / alpha)
    g = grad_from_weights(w, h, n)
    return val, g, c


def lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    try:
        val, _, _ = lse_value_grad(x, n, alpha)
    except Exception:
        return 1e100
    diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * n
    return float(val + mu * diff * diff / float(n * n))


def lbfgs_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    try:
        _, g, _ = lse_value_grad(x, n, alpha)
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=float))
    diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * n
    if mu:
        g = g + (2.0 * mu * diff / float(n)) * np.ones_like(x)
    return g


def smooth_sum_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)


def smooth_sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def slsqp_obj(x, n, alpha):
    try:
        val, _, _ = lse_value_grad(x, n, alpha)
    except Exception:
        return 1e100
    return float(val)


def slsqp_grad(x, n, alpha):
    try:
        _, g, _ = lse_value_grad(x, n, alpha)
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=float))
    return g


def active_sum_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x[1:]) - 0.5 * n)


def active_sum_jac(x, n):
    out = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    out[1:] = 1.0
    return out


def active_ineq_fun(x, n, ks):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    t = float(x[0])
    h = x[1:]
    out = np.zeros(len(ks), dtype=float)
    for r, k in enumerate(ks):
        val, _ = f_k_val_grad(h, n, int(k))
        out[r] = t - val
    return out


def active_ineq_jac(x, n, ks):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    h = x[1:]
    J = np.zeros((len(ks), n + 1), dtype=float)
    J[:, 0] = 1.0
    for r, k in enumerate(ks):
        _, g = f_k_val_grad(h, n, int(k))
        J[r, 1:] = -g
    return J


def active_obj(x, n):
    return float(x[0])


def optimize_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, alphas, mu, base_iter):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_from_h(h, n)
    cur_h = h.copy()
    cur_c = best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if not np.isfinite(cur_c):
            cur_h = project_h(best_h, n)
            cur_c = best_c
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbfgs_obj,
                cur_h,
                jac=lbfgs_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": base_iter, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-10, "maxls": 50},
            )
            cand = project_h(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                cc = c5_from_h(cand, n)
                if np.isfinite(cc):
                    if cc < best_c:
                        best_c = cc
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                    if cc < cur_c + 0.05:
                        cur_h = cand
                        cur_c = cc
        except Exception:
            pass

    if np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
        best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def optimize_slqsp_smooth(h, n, deadline, alpha, max_iter):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_from_h(h, n)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = (
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": smooth_sum_fun,
            "jac": smooth_sum_jac,
            "args": (n,),
        },
    )
    try:
        res = minimize(
            slsqp_obj,
            h,
            args=(n, alpha),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": max_iter, "ftol": 1e-13},
        )
        cand = project_h(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
            cc = c5_from_h(cand, n)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_h = cand
                best_c = cc
    except Exception:
        pass

    if np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
        best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def active_slqsp(h, n, deadline, active_tol, max_active, max_iter):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_from_h(h, n)

    if time.time() >= deadline or n < 8:
        return best_h, best_c

    c = corr_vec(h, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    idx = np.where(c >= m - active_tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    else:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
        if idx.size > max_active:
            idx = idx[:max_active]

    ks = (idx - (n - 1)).astype(int)
    x0 = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    x0[0] = m + 1e-5
    x0[1:] = h

    bounds = [(0.0, 2.0)] + [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = (
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": active_sum_fun,
            "jac": active_sum_jac,
            "args": (n,),
        },
        {
            "type": "ineq",
            "fun": active_ineq_fun,
            "jac": active_ineq_jac,
            "args": (n, ks),
        },
    )

    try:
        res = minimize(
            active_obj,
            x0,
            args=(n,),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": max_iter, "ftol": 1e-12},
        )
        cand = project_h(np.asarray(res.x[1:], dtype=float), n)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
            cc = c5_from_h(cand, n)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_h = cand
                best_c = cc
    except Exception:
        pass

    if np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
        best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def projected_gradient(h, n, deadline, alpha, lr_init, rng):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_from_h(h, n)
    curr_h = h.copy()
    curr_c = best_c

    lr = lr_init
    alpha = float(alpha)
    stagnant = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        try:
            _, g, c = lse_value_grad(curr_h, n, alpha)
        except Exception:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        curr_c = float(np.max(c))
        if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = curr_c
            best_h = curr_h.copy()

        g = g - np.mean(g)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-14:
            break
        g = g / gn

        step = lr
        improved = False
        for _ in range(18):
            cand = project_h(curr_h - step * g, n)
            cc = c5_from_h(cand, n)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < curr_c - 1e-15:
                curr_h = cand
                curr_c = cc
                improved = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if improved:
            stagnant = 0
            if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = curr_c
                best_h = curr_h.copy()
            lr = min(0.30, lr * 1.08)
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            lr *= 0.55
            alpha = min(20000.0, alpha * 1.4)

            if stagnant % 8 == 0:
                sigma = 0.02 / max(1.0, np.sqrt(float(n)))
                pert = project_h(curr_h + sigma * rng.normal(size=n), n)
                pc = c5_from_h(pert, n)
                if np.isfinite(pc) and pc < best_c + 0.04:
                    curr_h = pert
                    curr_c = pc

            if lr < 1e-11:
                break

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def local_pair_search(h, n, deadline, deltas, radius):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_from_h(h, n)

    for d in deltas:
        improved = True
        while improved and time.time() < deadline:
            improved = False
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                lo = max(0, i - radius)
                hi = min(n, i + radius + 1)
                for j in range(lo, hi):
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    if h[i] + d <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and h[j] - d >= -1e-12:
                        old_i = h[i]
                        old_j = h[j]
                        h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + d)
                        h[j] = max(0.0, h[j] - d)
                        c = c5_from_h(h, n)
                        if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-15:
                            best_c = c
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            improved = True
                        else:
                            h[i] = old_i
                            h[j] = old_j

                    if h[i] - d >= -1e-12 and h[j] + d <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
                        old_i = h[i]
                        old_j = h[j]
                        h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] - d)
                        h[j] = min(1.0, h[j] + d)
                        c = c5_from_h(h, n)
                        if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-15:
                            best_c = c
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            improved = True
                        else:
                            h[i] = old_i
                            h[j] = old_j

    best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
    best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def upsample(h, n, m):
    x_old = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    x_new = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(m))
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_h(y, m)


def positive_compose(total, parts, rng):
    total = int(total)
    if parts <= 0 or total <= 0:
        return [0] * max(0, parts)
    if parts > total:
        parts = total
    if parts == 1:
        return [total]

    cuts = np.sort(rng.randint(1, total, parts - 1))
    arr = np.concatenate(([0], cuts, [total]))
    return np.diff(arr).astype(int).tolist()


def compose_nonneg(total, parts, rng):
    total = int(total)
    if parts <= 0:
        return []
    if parts == 1:
        return [int(total)]
    if total <= 0:
        return [0] * parts

    cuts = np.sort(rng.randint(0, total + 1, parts - 1))
    arr = np.concatenate(([0], cuts, [total]))
    return np.diff(arr).astype(int).tolist()


def make_block_binary(n, rng, b=None):
    ones = n // 2
    zeros = n - ones
    if ones <= 0:
        return project_h(np.zeros(n, dtype=float), n)

    maxb = min(80, ones)
    if maxb <= 0:
        maxb = 1
    if b is None:
        b = int(rng.randint(1, maxb + 1))
    b = max(1, min(int(b), ones))

    blocks = positive_compose(ones, b, rng)
    gaps = compose_nonneg(zeros, b + 1, rng)

    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    pos = 0
    for r in range(b):
        if pos < n:
            pos += int(gaps[r])
            if pos < 0:
                pos = 0
        blen = int(blocks[r])
        if blen < 0:
            blen = 0
        if pos + blen > n:
            blen = max(0, n - pos)
        if blen > 0 and pos < n:
            h[pos:pos + blen] = 1.0
        pos += blen

    return project_h(h, n)


def make_sine_binary(n, rng):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5)):
        f = int(rng.randint(1, 6))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.6))
        y += a * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x + ph)

    med = float(np.median(y))
    h = (y > med).astype(float)
    return project_h(h, n)


def make_random_continuous(n, rng):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    y = 0.25 * rng.normal(size=n)
    y += 0.30 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(int(rng.randint(1, 4))) * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    h = np.clip(0.5 + y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_h(h, n)


def make_thue_binary(n, offset=0):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        x = i + int(offset)
        p = bin(x).count("1") & 1
        h[i] = float(p)
    return project_h(h, n)


def make_binary_exact(h, n, rng):
    if n < 2:
        return project_h(h, n)
    b = (np.asarray(h, dtype=float) >= 0.5).astype(float)
    target = n // 2
    cnt = int(b.sum())

    if cnt > target:
        ones = np.where(b > 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size:
            perm = rng.permutation(ones.size)
            take = min(cnt - target, ones.size)
            for idx in perm[:take]:
                b[ones[idx]] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        zeros = np.where(b < 0.5)[0]
        if zeros.size:
            perm = rng.permutation(zeros.size)
            take = min(target - cnt, zeros.size)
            for idx in perm[:take]:
                b[zeros[idx]] = 1.0

    return project_h(b, n)


def binary_score_vec(h, n):
    if n <= 900:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return corr_vec(h, n) * (0.5 * float(n))


def binary_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(binary_score_vec(h, n)) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def binary_active_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_active=16, max_candidates=4000, random_trials=30):
    if n < 20 or (n % 2) != 0:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, c5_from_h(h, n)

    h = make_binary_exact(h, n, rng).copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = binary_c5(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    stuck = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        sv = binary_score_vec(h, n)
        M = float(np.max(sv))
        tol = max(1.0, 0.03 * M)
        candidates_mask = sv >= (M - tol)
        if not np.any(candidates_mask):
            candidates_mask[int(np.argmax(sv))] = True

        idx = np.where(candidates_mask)[0]
        if idx.size > max_active:
            idx = idx[np.argsort(sv[idx])[::-1]][:max_active]
        ks = idx - (n - 1)

        cand_set = set()
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        for i in ones:
            ii = int(i)
            for k in ks:
                kk = int(k)
                j1 = ii + kk
                if 0 <= j1 < n and h[j1] < 0.5:
                    cand_set.add(ii * n + j1)
                j2 = ii - kk
                if 0 <= j2 < n and h[j2] < 0.5:
                    cand_set.add(ii * n + j2)

        if cand_set:
            cand_list = list(cand_set)
            if len(cand_list) > max_candidates:
                cand_list = rng.choice(cand_list, size=max_candidates, replace=False).tolist()
            else:
                rng.shuffle(cand_list)

            improved = False
            for code in cand_list:
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                i = code // n
                j = code % n
                if h[i] > 0.5 and h[j] < 0.5:
                    h[i] = 0.0
                    h[j] = 1.0
                    sc = float(np.max(binary_score_vec(h, n)))
                    cs = sc * dx
                    if cs < best_c - 1e-13:
                        best_c = cs
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        improved = True
                    elif cs <= best_c + 0.5 * dx and rng.rand() < 0.18:
                        pass
                    else:
                        h[i] = 1.0
                        h[j] = 0.0

            if improved:
                stuck = 0
                h = project_h(h, n)
                continue

        stuck += 1
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]

        if ones.size and zeros.size:
            best_rand_h = h.copy()
            best_rand_c = binary_c5(h, n)
            for _ in range(random_trials):
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                ii = int(rng.choice(ones))
                jj = int(rng.choice(zeros))
                h2 = h.copy()
                h2[ii] = 0.0
                h2[jj] = 1.0
                c2 = binary_c5(h2, n)
                if c2 < best_rand_c:
                    best_rand_c = c2
                    best_rand_h = h2

            if best_rand_c < best_c + 4.0 * dx:
                h = best_rand_h
            elif stuck > 8:
                h = make_binary_exact(best_h + 0.4 * rng.normal(size=n), n, rng)
                stuck = 0

        if stuck > 250:
            break

    best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
    best_c = binary_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def polish_candidate(h, n, deadline, rng, quick=False):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_from_h(h, n)
    t = time.time()
    remaining = deadline - t
    if remaining <= 0 or not np.isfinite(best_c):
        return best_h, float(best_c)

    if quick:
        dl = t + remaining * 0.45
        if dl > t + 3:
            if n <= 450:
                al = [300, 1500]
                it = 28
            elif n <= 900:
                al = [800, 3500]
                it = 18
            else:
                al = [1500]
                it = 10
            h2, c2 = optimize_lbfgs(h, n, dl, al, 10.0, it)
            if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_c = c2
            else:
                best_h = h2 if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_h.shape[0] + 1 else best_h
                if np.isfinite(c2):
                    h = project_h(h2, n)
                else:
                    h = project_h(best_h, n)

        t = time.time()
        remaining = deadline - t
        if remaining > 6:
            h2, c2 = projected_gradient(h, n, t + remaining, 3500, 0.035, rng)
            if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_c = c2

        best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
        best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, n)
        return best_h, float(best_c)

    if n <= 450:
        al = [50, 200, 800, 3000, 10000]
        it = 90
    elif n <= 850:
        al = [200, 1000, 4000]
        it = 45
    else:
        al = [500, 2500]
        it = 24

    dl = t + remaining * 0.33
    if dl > t + 4:
        h2, c2 = optimize_lbfgs(h, n, dl, al, 12.0, it)
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = c2
        h = project_h(h2 if np.isfinite(c2) else best_h, n)

    t = time.time()
    remaining = deadline - t
    if remaining > 10:
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient(h, n, t + remaining * 0.35, 700, 0.035, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = c2
        h = project_h(h2, n)

    t = time.time()
    remaining = deadline - t
    if remaining > 10:
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient(h, n, t + remaining * 0.55, 9000, 0.022, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = c2
        h = project_h(h2, n)

    t = time.time()
    remaining = deadline - t
    if n <= 360 and remaining > 18:
        dt = min(30.0, remaining * 0.4)
        h2, c2 = active_slqsp(h, n, t + dt, 0.004, 10, 350)
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = c2

    t = time.time()
    remaining = deadline - t
    if n <= 460 and remaining > 10:
        h2, c2 = local_pair_search(h, n, t + remaining * 0.7,
                                   [0.012, 0.006, 0.003, 0.0015],
                                   7 if n <= 360 else 4)
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = c2

    best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
    best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = np.inf
    best_n = 0

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
        n0 = 0

    if h0 is not None and n0 >= 8:
        h0 = project_h(h0, n0)
        c0 = c5_from_h(h0, n0)
        if np.isfinite(c0):
            best_h = h0.copy()
            best_c = float(c0)
            best_n = n0

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 300
        h0 = make_sine_binary(best_n, rng)
        h0 = project_h(h0, best_n)
        c0 = c5_from_h(h0, best_n)
        best_h = h0.copy()
        best_c = float(c0)
        best_n = best_n

    full_alloc = int(min(350, max(45, 0.31 * float(budget_s))))
    d1 = t0 + full_alloc
    if d1 > deadline - 170:
        d1 = deadline - 170
    if d1 > t0 + 30:
        h1, c1 = polish_candidate(best_h.copy(), best_n, d1, rng, quick=False)
        if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < best_c:
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_c = float(c1)
            best_n = len(h1)

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and best_n <= 420 and time.time() < deadline - 70:
        dbin = min(deadline - 90, time.time() + 120.0)
        hb, cb = binary_active_search(best_h, best_n, dbin, rng,
                                      max_active=20,
                                      max_candidates=5200 if best_n <= 320 else 3000)
        if np.isfinite(cb) and cb < best_c:
            best_h = hb.copy()
            best_c = float(cb)
            best_n = len(hb)

    if best_n <= 320:
        ns = [400, 600, 900]
    elif best_n <= 520:
        ns = [600, 900]
    else:
        ns = []

    for m in ns:
        tnow = time.time()
        if tnow >= deadline - 90:
            break
        if m > 1200:
            continue
        alloc = 130.0 if m <= 650 else 95.0
        dm = tnow + alloc
        if dm > deadline - 60:
            dm = deadline - 60
        if dm - tnow < 30:
            break

        hm = upsample(best_h, best_n, m)
        hp, cp = polish_candidate(hm, m, dm, rng, quick=True)
        if np.isfinite(cp) and cp < best_c:
            best_h = hp.copy()
            best_c = float(cp)
            best_n = len(hp)

        if best_n % 2 == 0 and best_n <= 420 and time.time() < deadline - 80:
            dbin2 = min(deadline - 80, time.time() + 45.0)
            hb2, cb2 = binary_active_search(project_h(best_h, best_n), best_n, dbin2, rng,
                                            max_active=14, max_candidates=2200)
            if np.isfinite(cb2) and cb2 < best_c:
                best_h = hb2.copy()
                best_c = float(cb2)
                best_n = len(hb2)

    tnow = time.time()
    if tnow < deadline - 45:
        nstart = best_n if 150 <= best_n <= 450 else 300
        if nstart < 160:
            nstart = 300
        if nstart % 2 == 1:
            nstart += 1

        starts = [make_sine_binary(nstart, rng)]
        for b in [1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21]:
            starts.append(make_block_binary(nstart, rng, b))
        starts.append(make_thue_binary(nstart, int(rng.randint(0, 32))))
        starts.append(make_random_continuous(nstart, rng))

        for hs in starts:
            if time.time() >= deadline - 25:
                break
            hs = project_h(hs, nstart)
            cs = c5_from_h(hs, nstart)
            if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs > best_c + 0.07:
                continue

            alloc = 22.0 if nstart <= 360 else 12.0
            dq = time.time() + alloc
            if dq > deadline - 20:
                dq = deadline - 20
            if dq - time.time() < 5:
                break

            hq, cq = polish_candidate(hs, nstart, dq, rng, quick=True)
            if np.isfinite(cq) and cq < best_c:
                best_h = hq.copy()
                best_c = float(cq)
                best_n = len(hq)

            if nstart % 2 == 0 and nstart <= 400 and time.time() < deadline - 25:
                db = min(deadline - 25, time.time() + 18.0)
                hbb, cbb = binary_active_search(hq, nstart, db, rng,
                                                max_active=12, max_candidates=1500)
                if np.isfinite(cbb) and cbb < best_c:
                    best_h = hbb.copy()
                    best_c = float(cbb)
                    best_n = len(hbb)

    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
        hf, cf = polish_candidate(best_h, best_n, deadline - 1.0, rng,
                                  quick=(budget_s < 700))
        if np.isfinite(cf) and cf < best_c:
            best_h = hf.copy()
            best_c = float(cf)
            best_n = len(hf)

    best_h = project_h(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, best_n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_n = 300
        best_h = project_h(np.full(best_n, 0.5, dtype=float), best_n)
        best_c = c5_from_h(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
